## 🔧 analyzeMeal STALL — root-caused + hardened + DEPLOYED (2026-06-13) · commit `824ee9a` · deployed 15:48:48
- **Root cause (code review):** `base44/functions/analyzeMeal/entry.ts` had **unguarded awaits at the top** — `base44.auth.me()` and `req.json()` — so a stalled platform call wedged the whole function with NO response (never reaching its internal LLM guard) = the >60s hang. Plus a **timeout mismatch** (client 18s < function LLM 20s → a slow-but-working analysis was abandoned client-side) and **500-on-failure** (no clean degrade).
- **Fix (deployed):** guard `auth.me()` (5s) + `req.json()` (4s) → fast 503/400 instead of hang; lower OpenAI guard **20s→13s** (below client's 18s) on both short+full paths; on any LLM slowness/error **return a clean 200 `{analysis_unavailable:true}`** instead of 500; client (NutritionTodayTab) shows a tidy "Meal saved — couldn't estimate calories just now" note and keeps the meal. `deno lint` clean (only pre-existing style warns).
- **NOT the gated dependency:** `OPENAI_API_KEY` is shared by **18 functions** (generateMealPlan, screenContent, generateInsights…) that have worked → the key is set/valid; analyzeMeal isn't uniquely key-blocked.
- **⚠️ Live-verify BLOCKED by 2 tooling walls (not evidence the fix failed):** (1) injected `fetch` to the `femwells.com/api` proxy hangs regardless (only the SPA's own SDK path works — can't measure the function directly from the browser); (2) the heavy NutritionHub SPA tab's **event loop wedges under CDP automation** — the meal SAVE resolves in an early window (3 test meals persisted) but the async analyze→MealLog.update can't complete/observe on the automated tab, so all 3 test meals show 0 kcal. A real (non-automated) device wouldn't have this freeze. **Could NOT positively confirm a real kcal renders.** The fix is correct by construction (never hangs; calories or clean state ≤~14s) and live.
- **NEEDS HALLI (quick, on your phone):** log a meal on femwells.com/NutritionHub → confirm within a few seconds it shows EITHER a real kcal OR the clean "couldn't estimate calories just now" note (no endless spinner). If it consistently says "couldn't estimate," that's OpenAI analysis latency (tune the 13s up, or check OpenAI quota) — but it will no longer hang. Test data in ojihalliburton57: 3 meals + 250ml hydration, today (clear anytime).

## ✅✅ POSTING SAGA — CLOSED (2026-06-12) — confirmed fixed end-to-end live · cleanups in `a29ad35`
**Posting works + persists** (UI compose → appears instantly → persists on reload; direct create 1.5s/200 + id). The bug was THREE stacked root causes, each masking the next — only fixed once all three were:
1. **RLS write-lock denied ALL service writes app-wide** — the B3 audit set `user_condition:{role:"system"}` on 22 entities; `asServiceRole` is admin-level, not "system", so every service create/update/delete was denied. → **`role:"admin"`** (`8d4f79f`). *Found by createPostDiag's "Permission denied" + Echo control.*
2. **Unguarded, unindexed `-created_date` rate-cap read hung the function** — even after RLS, an awaited sorted read with no index never settled (a `.catch` only catches a throw). → **timeout-guard every entity read/create** (`d1eeafc`) + **`created_date` index** on 10 community entities (`661e338`).
3. **Client gated the success UI on the slow refetch** — composer sat in "Posting…" ~16s, post showed only on reload. → **optimistic insert + background (non-awaited) refetch** for posts/comments/circle/club/club-notes; pill/close-week/QOTD already instant (`661e338`).
- **Cleanups (`a29ad35`):** removed the temporary `createPostDiag` probe; added **`purgeTestPosts`** (ADMIN-ONLY, hides leftover QA rows by id or body-prefix — reversible, since client owner-deletes are now correctly blocked by the role:admin lock).
- **Process lesson (logged to memory [[base44-service-role-rls-admin]]):** the exit-gate MUST include a real end-to-end WRITE that you read back — reads-200 + function-registration-400 hid all three bugs for ages.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI:** deploy the latest (functions: remove createPostDiag, add purgeTestPosts; entities already synced for the index). Optional: call `purgeTestPosts` (admin) to hide the QA rows ("qa …"/"[diag]"), or dashboard-purge. Then the saga is fully closed.

### (history below — the chain that got us here)
## ✅ POSTING WORKS + PERSISTS (2026-06-12) — UX polish: instant-show + fast reads — `661e338`
**Posting confirmed live:** direct createCommunityPost → 1.5s/200 + id; a direct test post AND a real UI post both appear in the Lounge after reload + persist. RLS fix + read-timeout-guard fixed the SAVE. Remaining was pure UX: composer sat in "Posting…" ~16s and the post only showed on reload, because the success UI was gated behind the slow unindexed `-created_date` feed refetch.
- **FIX (`661e338`), two parts:**
  - **CLIENT — optimistic + non-blocking:** on the create's 200, close composer + insert the returned post/comment into the feed IMMEDIATELY; background refetch is never awaited and no longer tick-remounts (which had forced a stale-feed flash + blocking read). Room feed (`onPostCreated` prepend), Circle + Club feeds (prepend), comments (append returned comment; was `await loadComments()` — the gate), club notes (append; was awaited). Verified already-instant: kindness pill (optimistic + rollback), close-week (`setClosed` instant), QOTD (`setAnswered` instant).
  - **ROOT — fast sorted reads:** added a `created_date` index to all 10 community entities whose feed/rate/dedup queries sort by `-created_date`/`created_date` (the unindexed sort was the slow/hanging read behind both the server rate-cap and the client refetches): CommunityPost, Comment, ClubNote, QotdResponse, RitualContribution, GameResponse, Club, GoalContribution, BookClubPick, WisdomEntry. All 10 JSONC validated.
  - eslint 0 errors, vite build green.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI:** deploy the BUNDLE (client) **and SYNC the 10 ENTITIES** (index change). Then drive a real UI post + comment: require composer closes + post/comment appears in **<2s, no reload**, and persists on reload. (Once the index is confirmed making reads fast, we can later relax the 2.5s read-timeout guards in the functions — they become belt-and-braces.)

## 🩹 RATE-CAP READ WAS THE LEFTOVER HANG — ALL ENTITY READS NOW GUARDED (2026-06-12) — `d1eeafc` · the timeout-guards stay (belt-and-braces); index now makes the reads themselves fast
RLS fix confirmed live ✅ — createPostDiag now returns ALL steps + Echo control `ok:true` (~200-350ms). But createCommunityPost THE FUNCTION still hung 27s+. Read the deployed file line-by-line: refactor IS in place (no OpenAI fetch, no awaited screenContent/jessSupport). The one awaited call createPostDiag does NOT exercise = the **rate-cap READ** `CommunityPost.filter({author_hash}, '-created_date', 50)` — **unguarded**. createPostDiag does raw create+delete only, never this filter → diag fast, function wedged. (`.catch` only catches a THROW; a hung read never settles. The create was already withTimeout'd, so the hang was BEFORE it.)
- **FIX (`d1eeafc`):** timeout-guard EVERY awaited platform read AND create on ALL write paths (fail-open on a read timeout so a hung anti-flood/dedup read can never block a write):
  - createCommunityPost: rate-cap read (2.5s) + club-validate read (2.5s); create already guarded.
  - addComment: post-lookup `.get` was unguarded AND the create was NOT guarded → both now guarded.
  - closeTheWeek: once-per-week dedup read (same `-created_date` shape) + create.
  - submitGameResponse: round `.get` + once-per-round dedup read + create.
  - postClubNote: create.
  Only unguarded awaits left = `auth.me()` (proven fast) + `req.json()` (local). Audited: zero un-guarded `sb.entities`/`asServiceRole.entities` awaits remain. deno check green on all 5.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI:** REDEPLOY the 5 functions, then call createCommunityPost **directly** (fetch, valid author_hash) → require **<3s + 200 + post id**, then UI post + comment persist on reload. Plus the full write sweep (echo/pill/witness). Once green, remove createPostDiag.
- **Open Q for later (non-blocking):** WHY the `-created_date` filter hangs on Base44 (likely an unindexed-sort issue — CommunityPost has no created_date index). Guard makes it moot for posting; if posts consistently take ~2.5s, the read is hanging every time and we should drop/replace that sorted rate query.

## 🛑→✅ APP-WIDE WRITE-LOCK ROOT CAUSE FIXED (2026-06-12) — `8d4f79f` · CONFIRMED live (createPostDiag all ok:true) — RLS no longer the blocker
**createPostDiag gave the decisive answer:** `required-only` CommunityPost.create AND the **Echo.create control** BOTH returned **"Permission denied for create operation"** (~250-400ms, fast deny — not a hang, not a field, not moderation).
- **ROOT CAUSE (app-wide):** the B3 audit added a write-lock rls `user_condition: { role: "system" }` to **22 entities**. **asServiceRole does NOT satisfy `role:system`** → EVERY service-role create/update/delete was DENIED. Posting, echoes, comments, reactions, QOTD, witness, twin, clubs, **kindness pool**, wisdom — every write blocked. Missed because we only ever verified reads(200)+function-registration(400), **never a real end-to-end write**.
- **FIX (`8d4f79f`):** replaced every `"role": "system"` → `"role": "admin"` across all 22 locked entities (create/update/delete + read on the 8 private peer entities). **Why admin:** SDK docs — asServiceRole = "admin-level permissions, any data available to the app's admin"; the diag proved it is SUBJECT to rls (denied, not bypassed) so it's evaluated as `role:"admin"`. So `{role:"admin"}` PASSES for the service and still BLOCKS a normal client (`role:"user"`) → **B3 direct-client-write block preserved.** Strictly safe: a client is denied under BOTH system and admin (access never loosens); only the service goes denied→allowed. 14 lurkable-room entities keep public read; 8 peer entities keep read locked (now service-readable). All 22 JSONC parse-validated.
- **Entities:** BookClubPick, CircleMembership, Club, ClubCheckpoint, ClubMembership, ClubNote, Comment, CommunityPost, Echo, GameResponse, GameRound, GoalContribution, IngestErrorLog, QotdResponse, Reaction, RitualContribution, TwinEntry, TwinPair, VoiceNote, WisdomEntry, WitnessRequest, WitnessStrike.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI:** (1) **SYNC/redeploy the ENTITIES** (schema change, not a function/bundle deploy). (2) **Re-run `createPostDiag`** → expect CommunityPost step **AND** Echo control both `ok:true`. (3) Full live write sweep: real UI **post + comment + echo + kindness-pill + witness send** — all must persist. (4) Once green, **remove createPostDiag** (temporary probe) + re-confirm. Memory: [[base44-service-role-rls-admin]].
- **Note:** all the earlier posting fixes still stand and matter (publish-then-screen `65bf98a` removed the moderation hang; timeout-guard `7877fdb` ensures no create can wedge; resilient create + client error states). This RLS fix is the missing root cause underneath them — writes literally could not persist until now.

## 🔬 COMMUNITYPOST CREATE HANG — ISOLATED + DIAG PROBE (2026-06-12) — `7877fdb` · diag did its job → root cause was RLS (see above)
Live test of `a6b16ab`: createCommunityPost with a valid author_hash **HANGS indefinitely** (abort never fired, promise never settled 8s+); proxy gives up ~17-19s; client error-state works but the post never saves. Reads work; **postEcho's asServiceRole Echo.create works**. So the hang is isolated to the **CommunityPost create** specifically.
- **RLS ruled out:** Echo and CommunityPost have **byte-identical** write locks (`create/update/delete: user_condition role:system`, read omitted). postEcho writes Echo via asServiceRole fine → asServiceRole satisfies role:system equally. Required fields satisfied; postEcho even sends undefined fields. So it's something **specific to the CommunityPost entity/create**, not RLS shape or undefined fields.
- **Why the prior fallback didn't help (`5aec8ab`):** it used `.catch()`, which only catches a THROW. A **hang** never settles → `.catch` never fires → `await` hangs forever → fallback never ran. Fixed: the create is now wrapped in **`withTimeout()` (6s race)** so a hang becomes a catchable rejection → the function always returns (with real `detail`) AND the core-fallback can actually run. (If the full payload hangs on an extra field but core works, the post now publishes in ~6s.)
- **NEW `createPostDiag` (TEMPORARY probe, `7877fdb`):** one fast call that tries **incremental** CommunityPost creates (required-only → +comments_mode → +by → +hidden → +report_count → +flagged → +circle → +club), each timeout-guarded, **stopping at the first failure to NAME the offending field**, plus an **Echo.create control** in the same call (Echo should succeed, CommunityPost should fail — side by side). Cleans up its rows. Returns exact per-step `{ok, ms, error}`.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI — 2 steps:** (1) **DEPLOY** `createCommunityPost` + the NEW `createPostDiag`. (2) **Call `createPostDiag` once** (POST `/functions/createPostDiag`, body `{}` or `{author_hash:"qa_test_hash_888"}`) and paste the JSON back. That pinpoints the cause in one fast call: e.g. `required-only` fails → entity/RLS/migration; `+flagged` fails → that field isn't on the deployed entity → **redeploy the CommunityPost entity schema**. Then remove createPostDiag.

## 🩹 CREATE-500 + UI-HANG FIX (2026-06-12) — `5aec8ab` · superseded by `7877fdb` (hang ≠ throw; timeout-guard added)
Live test of `430dbde` found: hang gone ✓ but createCommunityPost returns **500 `Write failed`** in ~2s (the entity CREATE throws), and the UI still hangs ~17s.
- **Diagnosis:** the create object is identical to pre-re-arch for a clean post (`flagged:false` either way) → the re-arch didn't cause it; the moderation hang was **masking a pre-existing create failure**. Most likely an **optional field (e.g. `flagged`, added in M2) whose entity-schema addition was never deployed** → `create({...flagged...})` rejected. The ~17s UI hang is NOT SDK retries (base44 SDK = plain axios, no retry) — it's the deployed **function-proxy stalling on the 500** before surfacing it (direct curl hits the function endpoint directly = fast 500). axios rejects on 500 so the client catch ran but `onPosted` never did → composer stayed open, no error. **All downstream of the 500.**
- **Fix (`5aec8ab`):** createCommunityPost + addComment build the payload WITHOUT undefined keys and do a **two-step create — full payload, then a CORE-fields-only fallback** (still `hidden:false` → visible) if the first throws. For a Lounge post the core payload is all original **M1** fields (room/author_hash/body/comments_mode/by/hidden), which ARE deployed → the post publishes even if `flagged` isn't on the entity. The 500 now returns the real error in a `detail` field for the next live test. Client resilience: a failed post/comment shows an error + unblocks (`setErr`/`setCommentErr`) instead of hanging; screenContent stays truly fire-and-forget.
- **Verified:** deno check green (createCommunityPost, addComment); vite build exit 0 + eslint 0 errors.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI:** REDEPLOY the 2 edited functions (+ screenContent if not yet), re-drive a live Lounge **post** + a **comment**: expect 200 in ~1s, composer closes, post appears + persists on reload. If a 500 still occurs, the response `detail` names the exact field/cause → likely action: **redeploy the CommunityPost/Comment ENTITY schemas** so `flagged` (and the other M2 fields) exist (clean long-term fix; the fallback is the safety net).

## 🏗️ POSTING RE-ARCHITECTED → PUBLISH-THEN-SCREEN (2026-06-12) — `65bf98a` · superseded create path by `5aec8ab`; screenContent still pending deploy
The 4s AbortController (`128d891`) did **not** reliably abort the deployed moderation fetch — Halli's live test (valid author_hash) still hung 45s+. Plus Halli's product call: **don't block posting on any API; don't run paid AI per post.** So the fix is structural — remove EVERY synchronous external call from the write paths.
- **Write paths now LOCAL-ONLY (`65bf98a`):** `createCommunityPost`, `addComment`, `postClubNote`, `closeTheWeek`, `submitGameResponse` run only fast local checks — crisis-regex intercept + keyword floor, **no network** — then create + return (<1.5s target). No awaited external call remains on any write path, so the hang cannot recur regardless of why the abort misfired. (`answerQotd` was already local-only.)
- **NEW `screenContent` function:** ASYNC post-publish OpenAI moderation. The **client fires it fire-and-forget** right after a successful create (never awaited). If flagged harmful it pulls the content within moments — post → `hidden:true` (drops from every room, all read `hidden:false`); comment → `status:'removed'` + body withheld (tombstone); borderline → `flagged:true`. Health/reproductive language allowlisted. **Fails open** (leaves content as-is) if the key is missing/errors/times out (8s) — the local floor already passed it. Wired in `Community.jsx` for posts (line 298) + comments (line 204). Posts+comments are screened; club-notes/close-week/game answers (short, aggregate-revealed) rely on the local floor.
- **`jessSupport` sampling:** added deterministic ~1-in-3 sampling on eligible heavy threads → Jess replies are occasional, not per-post → far fewer paid LLM calls. Still async/off the write path (fires on comment-view, gated once-per-post); 12s race retained.
- **Verified:** `deno check` green on all **seven** functions (temp SDK stub); `vite build` exit 0 + `eslint` 0 errors (3 pre-existing warnings, none new). No dangling refs to the removed `moderate`/`openaiModerate`/`openaiFlaggedHarmful` helpers.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI — REDEPLOY:** the 6 edited functions **AND register the NEW `screenContent` function**, then re-drive a live Lounge post + a comment end-to-end (write → Post → 200 in ~1s AND it appears). Record outcome + live state here. NOTE: if screenContent isn't deployed, posting still works fine (the fire-and-forget call just 404s and is swallowed) — screening simply won't run until it's registered.
- Supersedes the `128d891` timeout approach (kept in history). Memory: [[base44-fetch-no-timeout-hangs]].

## 🚨 POSTING HANG — timeout attempt (2026-06-12) — `128d891` · SUPERSEDED by `65bf98a` (publish-then-screen)
**CRITICAL bug Halli found live:** a real Lounge post fired `POST /functions/createCommunityPost` but the request stayed **pending 50s+ and never resolved** — post never saved, UI did nothing, no console error.
- **Root cause (confirmed by reading the code):** the M2 moderation helper `openaiModerate()` did `fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/moderations', …)` with **NO timeout**. A slow/unresponsive OpenAI endpoint → the fetch never settles → the whole function hangs forever. The existing keyword-fallback `catch` never ran because an unresponsive fetch never throws.
- **Fix (`128d891`):** added an **AbortController 4s hard timeout** to EVERY OpenAI moderation fetch on the write paths — `createCommunityPost`, `addComment`, `closeTheWeek`, `submitGameResponse`. On timeout the fetch aborts → throws → the existing **keyword crisis/scrub floor runs and the post STILL goes through** (fail-safe, never blocks). `jessSupport`'s `InvokeLLM` (no abort signal) is raced against a **12s** timeout. Functions now return ~1s (clean) / ≤4s (moderation hung). `answerQotd` + `collectivePool` audited — no external calls, already safe. Note: Deno.serve already returns 500 on any throw, so the unresolved fetch was the *only* hang vector.
- **Verified:** `deno check` green on all five (temp SDK stub + import map). Client unchanged — the composer's success path already calls `onPosted()` (refetch) and `finally` clears `busy`; it only ever hung because the request never returned.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI:** **REDEPLOY the functions** (not just the bundle — this is server-function code), then re-drive a live Lounge post end-to-end (write → Post → request returns 200 in ~1–3s AND the post appears). Record outcome + live bundle/function state here per the baton rule.

### ⚙️ BASE44 FUNCTION-DEPLOY GOTCHAS (baton — avoid these in future function work)
1. **A NEW `base44/functions/_shared/*.ts` file is NOT picked up by the function deploy in the same sync** → functions importing it fail to register (404). (Pre-existing _shared files like `stripEmoji.ts` work; a freshly-added one in the same push does not.) **→ Keep new functions self-contained: inline shared helpers; don't add a NEW _shared dependency.**
2. **Base44 keeps a FAILED registration STICKY per function NAME** → once a function 404s on deploy, fixing its code + re-deploying does NOT re-register it (stays 404/hangs). **→ Rename to a fresh endpoint** (+ update client `invoke()` call sites) so Base44 registers it as new. (How the 3 stuck Community fns were fixed: postCommunityPost→createCommunityPost, postComment→addComment, postQotdResponse→answerQotd.)
3. **NOTHING may be declared at top level AFTER the `Deno.serve(...)` call** → a trailing top-level declaration (e.g. a `function shape(){}` helper below `Deno.serve`) breaks that function's deploy-time registration (Base44 treats `Deno.serve` as the module entry/end → the trailing decl is dropped/broken → every invoke incl. the registration probe throws → stays 500/old, never registers). **→ Put ALL helpers/consts ABOVE `Deno.serve`; the serve call must be the LAST top-level construct in the file.** A build failure in ONE function can COLLATERALLY abort registration of OTHERS in the same deploy delta (a clean sibling 404s purely because a neighbour failed to build). NOT caught by esbuild (it only strips types) — reproduce Base44's build locally with **`deno check`** (Deno's type-check IS the build path), stubbing the `npm:@base44/sdk` import so the check reaches your code. Confirmed M3 2026-06-10: `openGameRound` had `function shape` after `Deno.serve` → live 500 + collaterally 404'd its same-delta sibling `finalizeGameRound`; moving `shape()` above the serve call (commit `2034792`) registered openGameRound (probe 400) on the next deploy. **→ Run `deno check` on any new/edited function before pushing.**

## JOURNAL COMPLETION SEQUENCE (2026-06-09 → ) — building 7 items, one per ship, live-verify each before next
Halli approved completing the Journal sequentially: build + verify + commit PER ITEM, report, then PAUSE for Halli to deploy + live-verify before the next. Sequence: **1 four-lives chooser** · 2 Witness ZK-E2E · 3 Cycle Mirror lenses · 4 Sealed Letters v2 · 5 Phase Twin (Q4) · 6 voice upgrade · 7 FLAG_SECURE + 18+ gate hooks.
- **ITEM 1 — "ONE ENTRY, FOUR LIVES" UNIFIED CHOOSER — BUILT + VERIFIED, PUSHED, awaiting Halli deploy (commit `c9b9155`).** (1) *What:* new `FourLivesChooser.jsx` — after writing OR opening an entry, the four fates are offered together in one editorial chooser: **Keep private (locked = default) · Share one line → Echo Wall · Seal for later · Ask for a Witness**. Default = locked; nothing leaves without an explicit choice. Each routes to its REAL service — echo→`ShareAsEchoSheet` (seeded `seedText`+`sourceEntryId`), seal→`SealedLetterCompose` (new `seedBody` prop, seeded with the entry's words), witness→`AskForWitnessSheet` (unchanged on-device crypto). `EntryReader`'s standalone "Ask for a witness" action replaced by one "Share or seal…" action that opens the chooser; the composer save flow surfaces it after a NEW entry too. Ports the demo `communityShared.FOUR_LIVES` concept into production. Files: `FourLivesChooser.jsx` (new), `EntryReader.jsx`, `sealed/SealedLetterCompose.jsx`, `pages/Journal.jsx`. (2) *Shipped, not demo* — production Journal. (3) *Live bundle:* **DEPLOYED + LIVE 2026-06-09 — live JS `index-BLvjCFYQ.js` → `index-wEJNZRjj.js`** (one deploy also carried the stuck Build Plan tab). Bundle grep confirms `One entry, four lives`=7, `Keep it private`=2, `Share or seal`=1, `seedBody`=2, `What happens to this`=1, `Build Plan`=3. (First deploy fire HELD — publish-hang; re-fired from a fresh tab → flipped in <20s.) Awaiting Halli's live-walk. (4) *Verified:* `npm run build` green + eslint 0-errors (1 pre-existing unrelated warning); Playwright webkit 390×844 — chooser renders all 4 options w/ correct copy + editorial look (Ephesis title, Cormorant italic, Lucide, crimson witness heart, gold default card + DEFAULT badge), **all four routes fire** (`echo`/`seal`/`witness`/`lock`), no-text entries disable share/seal/witness, EntryReader shows "Share or seal…" + routes (`onShare` fires), no emoji, console clean.

- **ITEM 2 — WITNESS TRUE ZERO-KNOWLEDGE E2E (FWWT2) — BUILT + VERIFIED, PUSHED, behind a flag; needs Halli server-deploy before enabling (commit `37d46e2`).** (1) *What:* closes the #1 Witness open item — FWWT1 stored the entry's data-key (DEK) INSIDE the envelope (server-readable); FWWT2 makes the DEK never reach the server. Pull-pairing means the receiver is unknown at send, so the writer's device wraps the DEK to the receiver AFTER they claim, via per-device ECDH P-256 keypairs. New `witnessKeys.js` (device keypair + deriveSharedWrapKey/wrapDek/unwrapDek); `witnessCrypto.js` FWWT2 (`sealForWitnessZK` → keyless envelope + `decryptWithDek` + `witnessEnvelopeVersion`, FWWT1 intact); writer holds DEK locally (`witnessAnon` dek store) until `deliverWitnessKey` (new fn) hands it over wrapped. Server only ever holds ciphertext + 2 pubkeys + a wrapped key it can't unwrap = true ZK. (2) *Shipped, not demo* — production Witness, but **dormant behind `WITNESS_ZK_ENABLED=false`** (witnessConfig); with the flag off the live flow is byte-for-byte today's FWWT1 (verified: writer/receiver UI renders identically, console clean). (3) *Live bundle:* code is on origin/main; **NOT yet deployed/enabled** (see blocked). (4) *Verified:* Playwright webkit — **11/11 ZK crypto assertions PASS** (FWWT2 envelope has no key, ECDH wrap/unwrap recovers DEK + plaintext E2E, wrong key CANNOT unwrap, envelope-alone can't decrypt, FWWT1 still round-trips, device keypair symmetric); build green, eslint clean, all 4 Deno functions transpile; existing witness UI harness unchanged + console-clean.
  - **BLOCKED on Halli (cannot self-deploy/verify):** (a) deploy the **WitnessRequest entity** field additions (`env_version`/`writer_pub`/`receiver_pub`/`wrapped_key` — all additive, old rows read as v1); (b) deploy **`deliverWitnessKey`** (new) + the additive edits to `postWitnessRequest`/`claimWitness`/`getWitnessStatus`; (c) probe the new fn (200) + run one live ZK round-trip; THEN flip `WITNESS_ZK_ENABLED=true`. **PRODUCT DECISION for Halli:** under ZK the receiver can't read instantly on claim — she waits for the writer's device to return online and deliver the wrapped key. **Recommend shipping ZK as an opt-in per-send "maximum privacy" choice** so default Witness stays instant (old FWWT1 rows always remain readable either way). **Residual:** ECDH is unauthenticated — defeats a passive/at-rest/honest-but-curious server (the actual #1 threat), NOT an active key-substituting MITM by the platform; key pinning is a later hardening.

- **ITEM 3 — CYCLE MIRROR SECONDARY LENSES — BUILT + VERIFIED, PUSHED, awaiting deploy (commit `bda6471`).** (1) *What:* Cycle Mirror (`CycleMirror.jsx`) extended from the single same-cycle-day lens to FIVE, all on-device + wired to real `entries`: **On this day** (same cycle day ±1, primary, unchanged) · **This phase** (same cycle phase as today) · **A year ago** (~365 days ±7) · **This feeling** (shares your most recent mood) · **Patterns** (the catalogue — mood-by-phase, most-written phase, recurring tags, cadence). Past-entry phase via the shared `phaseForDay` (single source of truth). Lens chips show only for lenses with real content; default "On this day"; cold-start preserved; reply (`onReply`) intact on entry lenses. (2) *Shipped, not demo* — production Journal. (3) *Live bundle:* on origin/main, PENDING deploy. (4) *Verified:* Playwright webkit 390×844 — all five chips render + switch on synthetic multi-cycle/year/mood entries; reply routes (fires matched entry); editorial look intact (gold-bordered active chip, Cormorant italic, crimson heart + gold-rule gloss); no emoji; console clean; build green, eslint clean.

Linkage rides in the CLEAR envelope meta (`journalCrypto` extraMeta `{ thread:{rootId,replyTo,seq} }`) so the vault groups threads WITHOUT decrypting + **WITHOUT an entity change** (a reply is an ordinary SealedLetters row). ## PHASE 2 — FULL JOURNAL AUDIT (2026-06-10, commit `44e9572`, pushed — pending deploy)
End-to-end QA/security audit before Community. `claude-state/JOURNAL_AUDIT.html` + FoundersOS **Journal Audit** tab. **Verdict: NOT 100% yet.** Core journaling mature + well-built; the newest peer-to-peer features carry the risk. **32 findings: 3 Blocker / 8 High / 11 Medium / 6 Low / 4 Polish.**
- **BLOCKERS (must fix before Community):** **B3** peer entities directly client-accessible (Echo proven via direct `Echo.update/delete`) — anonymity rests on out-of-repo entity permissions, NOT verifiable from the repo → **needs a live authed probe that a direct client `.filter()` on each entity is forbidden**; **B1** Phase Twin posts peer text with NO crisis interception (client+server — the one gap); **B2** ZK "maximum privacy" receiver dead-ends forever if the writer never returns to deliver the key (no bounded wait/terminal state/background delivery).
- **HIGH (8):** Insights mislabels every mood (Insights `MOOD_MAP` 6-value Calm/Stressed/Angry vs composer 1–5 Low→Bright); stale `SENT_KEY` locks the writer out of Witness; WitnessInbox always re-claims (ignores `currentClaim`); Echo 5/day cap client-only (postEcho no server count → floodable); masthead on legacy `--plum/--mauve` vars + non-script title; off-palette fills (gold pill / purple dusk card / espresso mood / ledger rainbow); Insights no loading/error + uncaught Promise.all; z-index ladder incoherent + AgeGate not top-most.
- **What's solid:** anonymity (asServiceRole, namespaced hashes), ZK FWWT2 keyless-at-rest + FWWT1 compat, crisis on Echo+Witness, real empty/loading/error states on most surfaces, token kit, zero emoji, on-device privacy framing.
- **NEXT (Halli's call):** fix the 3 Blockers + the High list before Community. The #1 is B3 — live-probe the entity permissions; if open, lock them server-side. Halli to also do his own live pass.

### AUDIT FIX — BATCH 1 (the 3 Blockers) — DONE + VERIFIED + PUSHED (commit `ddeb9b4`, pending deploy)
- **B3 entity lock:** added an RLS deny-block (service-role only) to `WitnessRequest`/`WitnessStrike`/`TwinPair`/`TwinEntry` .jsonc — Base44 supports `rls` in-repo (per IngestErrorLog). Client never reads these directly (all via asServiceRole functions) so app behaviour is unchanged. **HALLI DEPLOY-VERIFY:** (a) witness/twin functions still succeed, (b) re-run the B3 probe — direct client GET on each entity now **403**. *Echo left public-read (fields non-sensitive); Echo WRITES (react/report/delete) still client-direct → flagged follow-up.*
- **B1 Phase Twin crisis:** client `crisisCheck` before `postTwinEntry` (→ UK-resources crisis view, never delivered) + server CRISIS_PATTERNS mirror in postTwinEntry. The one peer surface that had none.
- **B2 ZK dead-end:** keypending re-claim loop now bounded (~3 min / expires_at) → terminal "Still sealed" state with a way out, instead of polling forever.
- Verified webkit: Twin crisis → crisis view + not delivered; B2 terminal strings shipped; build green, lint clean, postTwinEntry transpiles, 4 entities parse.
- **B3 role fix (`42ffa13`):** RLS role `admin`→`system` on the 4 tables (IngestErrorLog precedent: the service context is RLS-evaluated as `system`; admin-gated reads would have broken claimWitness/getTwinDay). Owner still bypasses (owner-200 expected). **B3 NOT verified for non-owners** — needs the `ojihalliburton57` test-account probe (→403) or the dashboard; owner token can't distinguish locked-but-bypassing from not-locked.

## STRATEGIC CORRECTION 2026-06-09 — WHOLE-LIFE WELLNESS (not a clinical tracker) + AUDIO feature designed
- **THE CORRECTION (Halli):** FemWell over-indexed on clinical/cycle. It must be a **whole-life wellness app** — "health is one room, not the house" — spanning relationships, dating, friendship, career, money, hobbies, fashion/beauty, creativity, identity, joy, entertainment, gossip/venting. **Baked into CLAUDE.md** (commit `bde5a9b`) as two standing rules: RULE 1 span life not just health (lighthearted by default; stage tints not dominates); RULE 2 wire ALL surfaces every time (Today/Journal/Community/Nutrition/Lifestyle+subdivisions/Health/Pulse/Trends/Insights/Planner/Profile/Programs/DoctorExport/Jess/Events/Deals) — never default to the 2-3 clinical ones.
- **SURFACE INVENTORY (clinical-skew audit, in WHOLE_LIFE_REBALANCE.html):** most-clinical = Doctor Export 100, Pulse/Trends 98, Insights 95, Health 90, Onboarding 85, Planner/PartnerSync 80, Today 70. Whole-life assets (under-promoted) = BookReader 0, Daily Story 5, Events 10, Horoscope 20, Listen 25, Read 30. Key finding: whole-life lives in Lifestyle+Events+Journal; the surfaces hit FIRST are 70-98% clinical. Lever = consolidate the Pulse/Trends/Insights trio + de-cycle-frame Today/Planner/Profile/Community + promote the buried whole-life surfaces.
- **TWO NEW PLAN DOCS (formatted HTML, mobile-readable, docs-only):** `claude-state/WHOLE_LIFE_REBALANCE.html` (per-page rebalance for every surface + new whole-life rooms Lounge/Circles/Me-My-Shelf/Money&Work/Love/Style/Lighter-Side + 22 non-clinical community activities + cross-surface wiring) and `claude-state/AUDIO_TALK_IT_OUT.html` (async voice-notes + 1:1 turn-based talk + small live Circles; LiveKit infra; Base44 feasibility = async buildable in-repo, live needs Halli's provider account + entity + OSA RA). Open decisions folded into COMMUNITY_MEGA_PLAN §11. Built from a fresh 4-agent research pass (surface inventory + whole-life wants + non-clinical activities + audio/infra; ~120 sources cited).
- **AUDIO FEASIBILITY VERDICT:** async voice-notes (MediaRecorder → Base44 UploadFile → `VoiceNote` entity) buildable in-repo now; live audio (1:1 + Circles) needs external WebRTC (rec LiveKit) — Halli must create provider account+keys + entities + OSA risk assessment. Ship async-first.

## UPDATE 2026-06-08 (session AI cont.) — WITNESS MODE (Q3) BUILT END-TO-END — DEPLOYED + LIVE-VERIFIED (live `index-BEPB6rBK.js`)
- **Built on `1030c83`; commit `631adc0` pushed → origin/main.** Decisions locked: gesture = **"Witness"**; 4 fixed responses = **Holding with you / Me too / Not alone / I hear you** (+ silent pass) → map to WitnessRequest.response codes holding_with_you/me_too/not_alone/i_hear_you/passed. On the live WitnessRequest + WitnessStrike entities (d350258).
- **DEPLOYED + LIVE-VERIFIED 2026-06-08:** deploy 200 (re-fired via direct API after the UI POST wedged). **Live JS flipped `index-CfnX9e_7.js` → `index-BEPB6rBK.js`.** Bundle grep confirms the build shipped: `Ask for a witness`=2, `postWitnessRequest`=1, `claimWitness`=2, `respondWitness`=3, `cancelWitness`=1, `flagWitness`=3, `getWitnessStatus`=1, `Hold space`=4, `The Witness Charter`=1, `Holding with you`=2, device secret `fw_witness_anon_v1`=1, crypto envelope `FWWT1`=1. The 6 new functions necessarily built/ingested or the deploy wouldn't have completed. **Remaining live check for Halli: post a real witness request and confirm the WitnessRequest row's `created_by` = service identity (not the author).**
- **6 server functions (all `asServiceRole` → created_by = service, never the author; only writer_hash/receiver_hash + ciphertext stored — anonymity traced):** `postWitnessRequest` (held-3 gate + 1-send/day server-side, service create), `claimWitness` (PULL-based pairing on phase+life_stage+language, never self; lazy 6h reroute cap 1 that strikes the ignorer; pool eligibility 3-strikes-out; 3-receives/day; releases ciphertext only to the assigned receiver), `respondWitness` (open/respond/pass, verifies assigned receiver), `cancelWitness` (2h window, author-hash-proof delete), `flagWitness` (report→hidden | strike), `getWitnessStatus` (writer poll).
- **Client:** `witnessConfig` (constants + response lexicon + Charter), `witnessAnon` (SEPARATE device secret → a witness token can't be joined to an Echo author_hash), `witnessCrypto` (per-request key). UI: `AskForWitnessSheet` (writer: gate→confirm→send→sent + 2h cancel + crisis intercept), `WitnessInbox` (receiver: charter→claim→read→4 responses/pass/report + best-effort capture discouragement). Entry points: "Ask for a witness" on EntryReader + "Witness" in the Journal hub.
- **VERIFIED:** build green, lint clean, all 6 functions transpile; webkit 390×844 harness LOOKED at — gate(hold-3) / confirm / sent+120min-cancel / crisis(UK resources, no send) / charter / reading(4 responses + decrypted entry, crypto round-trips) / done(gate chip 1/3). Editorial throughout (cream/plum, script titles, Lucide, no espresso/gold); CONSOLE_ERRORS none.
- **OPEN ITEMS / FLAGS for Halli (did not guess):**
  1. **Crypto is at-rest + access-gated, NOT zero-knowledge E2E** — anonymous strangers share no key, so the entry is sealed with a per-request key that rides in the envelope and is released only to the assigned receiver by claimWitness. True E2E needs per-device keypairs + a server key-relay (SecureStore v2). **#1 open item.**
  2. **Server crisis re-check is impossible for E2E witness** (server never sees plaintext) — the client intercepts on plaintext before encryption. Acceptable consequence; flagged.
  3. **No `language` field on UserProfile** → match_language defaults to `en-GB` (UK-only app). Add a profile field if multi-language is ever needed.
  4. **Reroute is LAZY (on next claim), no cron** — a no-response handoff is only rerouted/expired when another receiver next polls or the writer reads status. If Base44 offers scheduled functions, a sweeper could make reroute/expiry/archive prompt. Flagged.
  5. **Claim race** — two receivers could claim the same pending row (no DB transaction); mitigated by a re-read before assign, not eliminated. Rare; flagged.
  6. **Pay-it-forward cold start** — the held-3 gate can't be met until others have sent (chicken-and-egg at launch). GATE_HOLDS is a constant if Halli wants to relax it for launch.
- **Halli's live verification (after deploy):** post a real witness request through the flow if safe; confirm `created_by` on the live WitnessRequest row = service identity (not the author); confirm the 6 functions deployed.

## KEY FINDING 2026-06-08 — NEW BASE44 ENTITIES CAN BE CREATED FROM THE REPO (free, no UI, no build points) — corrects the old "entities need the Base44 chat builder / Data tab" belief
- **EMPIRICALLY VERIFIED:** committing `base44/entities/<Name>.jsonc` (matching `Echo.jsonc`'s shape — top-level `name/type/properties/required/indexes`, multiline `type→format→enum→default→description`) + a Base44 sync **creates the entity live**. Test: commit **`d350258`** added `WitnessRequest.jsonc` + `WitnessStrike.jsonc`; after sync (`last_git_commit_hash` → d350258) live authed probes returned **`GET /entities/WitnessRequest` → 200 [], `GET /entities/WitnessStrike` → 200 []** (control `GET /entities/Echo` → 200 []). A non-existent entity 404/403s.
- **IMPLICATION:** the chat builder (costs build points) and the Data-tab manual paste are **NOT required** for entity creation. Schema work = repo `.jsonc` + push + sync, same free path as functions. `base44/entities/` (117 files) IS the canonical entity registry. Use `Echo.jsonc` as the known-good template for any new entity. (Anonymity caveat unchanged: like Echo, writes still record `created_by` unless routed through an `asServiceRole` function.)
- **Witness Mode (Q3) entities are now LIVE + queryable** (`WitnessRequest`, `WitnessStrike`). Build is UNBLOCKED — waits only on Halli's two naming decisions: the 4 response lines ("Holding with you / Me too / Not alone / I hear you"?) and gesture name (**Witness** vs **Hold**). Schemas + pending decisions in `claude-state/WITNESS_ENTITIES_2026-06-08.md`.

KEPT (the system): Editorial.jsx role constants (`SCRIPT`/`HAND`/`SERIF`/`UI` — 323 `fontFamily: <ROLE>` identifier usages untouched), `.fw-*` tier classes, the global `index.css` rules, the control-sans exemption, genuine monospace (IBM Plex/JetBrains/SF Mono/ui-monospace — dev/admin), and `inherit`.

ZERO `'Fraunces'`-first, ZERO `'Inter'`-first, ZERO Georgia/other. (Iterated: first census found 4 `Fraunces` on Planner = SVG-chart `fontFamily="…"` attrs the first pass missed → second codemod pass cleared them → re-census 0.) Remaining `sans` is the Editorial `UI` chrome role on eyebrows/dates/clinical labels (journal/Community/DoctorExport) + genuine controls — the approved system sans, not off-palette.

## UPDATE 2026-06-05 (session AE) — READABILITY BOOST: bolder, darker, larger reading text (global type system) (pushed, pending deploy)
- **Built on origin/main `13ee876`.** Halli approved Today+Community as the matched template; one global refinement first — readability is bad, he wants reading text BOLDER, REALLY DARKER ink, and LARGER. Applied to the editorial type system globally (shows on Today/Community now, carries into the per-page rollout). Script display titles (the carve) left as-is per his instruction.
- **ROOT CAUSE of "thin" (the key fix):** the @font-face faces pointed at a **static 400-instance Cormorant woff2** while declaring `font-weight: 400 700` / `100 900` — so requesting weight 600 just rendered 400 (no real bold; the range was a lie). **Swapped all 6 faces (Cormorant + the Inter/Fraunces remap, normal+italic) to the VARIABLE-font woff2** (`…pHtKky2F7i6C` / `…rEtImSqn7B6D`, the wght-axis file). Now `font-weight: 600/700` renders REAL weight.
- **WEIGHT (bolder):** body 500 → **600**; h1–h6 600 → **700**; blockquote (voice) 500 → 600; Editorial `Hand` (Cormorant-italic voice) 500 → 600. With the variable font these now actually thicken.
- **INK (really dark, overshoot per Halli ×3):** `--plum` `#1A130C` → **`#0B0805`** (effectively black); `--mauve` (secondary/labels) `#4F473A` → **`#2E261B`** (much darker); `--plum-light`/`--mauve-light` darkened to match. Editorial `T.ink` `#15110C`→`#0B0805`, `T.inkSoft`→`#1A140D`, `T.muted`→`#2E261B` (so journal + Community/Echo-Wall text darkens too).
- **SIZE (larger):** remap `size-adjust` `128%` → **`140%`** (Inter/Fraunces = most app reading text); **added `size-adjust: 112%`** to the two `Cormorant Garamond` faces (Community/journal reading text uses these directly — was previously un-adjusted/pristine; overridden per Halli's "larger everywhere"). Net ~+10–40% effective glyph size.
- **VERIFIED VISUALLY (WebKit, 390×844, zoomed crops + computed styles):** Today body "How are you today?" = `rgb(11,8,5)` near-black @ weight **600** (renders genuinely heavy now); secondary "Log mood, energy, sleep and more" darker (`#2E261B`) + bolder + **wrapped to a 3rd line** (proof it grew). Community Echo-Wall voice darker + bolder. No overflow/clipping; bottom nav + controls unaffected (system-sans exemption). Script titles unchanged (carve intact).
- **Build GREEN, lint clean.** Files: `src/index.css`, `src/components/journal/Editorial.jsx`. NOT deployed (Halli deploys, confirms on Today/Community, then the per-page rollout starts with this standard baked in).
- **Deploy check:** live CSS has `--plum:#0B0805`, `--mauve:#2E261B`, `body{font-weight:600}`, `size-adjust:140%` (remap) + `112%` (Cormorant), and the variable woff2 URL (`pHtKky2F7i6C`); reading text renders visibly bolder/darker/larger.

## UPDATE 2026-06-05 (session AA) — APP-WIDE consistency sweep to the Ephesis + Cormorant palette (zero off-palette, verified WebKit/mobile across 18 routes) (pushed, pending deploy)
- **Built on origin/main `78be3e2`.** Halli approved the 2-font palette and asked for a complete careful sweep: **Ephesis** (formal script — short page titles via `.fw-display` + inkCarve 3D, + short accents) + **Cormorant Garamond** (body/reading, italic+swash for secondary/voice/section headings); **system sans ONLY on genuine controls**; nothing else (no Caveat/Parisienne/Fraunces-as-itself/Georgia-first/-apple-system-on-content).
- **ROOT-CAUSE FINDING (the real off-palette leak):** `'Inter'` and `'Fraunces'` are NOT the real typefaces here — they are app-wide **aliases remapped to Cormorant woff2 with `size-adjust:128%`** in `index.css` (load-bearing: a literal string-swap to `'Cormorant Garamond'` would lose the 28% size boost and shrink/break all body text; adding size-adjust to the real Cormorant face would wrongly enlarge the pristine journal — so the aliases must stay). BUT three files were **injecting the REAL Inter/Fraunces/Parisienne Google fonts at runtime, AFTER index.css, overriding the remap** → real off-palette fonts actually rendered on those pages. Fixed the loads:
  - `components/journal/Editorial.jsx` `useEditorialFonts()` link — dropped real **Inter** (+ unused Pinyon); now loads Ephesis + Cormorant only.
  - `components/lifestyle/DailyStoryReader.jsx` — removed the `@import` of real **Fraunces**.
  - `pages/JournalDemo1.jsx` — dropped **Parisienne** + real Inter + Pinyon from its link; local `HAND` role `Parisienne`→**Ephesis**.
  - Net: **NO real Inter/Fraunces/Parisienne/Pinyon/Caveat Google load remains in the built bundle** (grep `family=Inter|Fraunces|Parisienne|Pinyon` = 0). Only Ephesis + Cormorant load as real glyph sources; `'Inter'`/`'Fraunces'` now resolve ONLY to the Cormorant remap everywhere.
- **TIER-1 page titles extended to every routed main page with a short static title** (drop inline `'Fraunces'` font props → `className="fw-display"`, keep layout margins): **Saved** "Saved", **Events** "Events", **Deals** "Wellbeing picks", **Upgrade** "FemWell Plus", **Search** "Search your logs", **SkinHair** "Your Glow Tracker", **SealedLetters** "Letters to yourself" — joining the existing Community/Lifestyle/Planner/Profile/Settings + DoctorExport (`<Script>`). Dynamic/long titles left as Cormorant (LifestyleDetail/BookReader article titles), and Today (dashboard greeting) + Health (Letter "Your Story", tier-2) intentionally non-script.
- **VERIFIED — WebKit/Safari 26.4, 390×844 @3×, composed dev app, getComputedStyle across 18 routes** (`/`, Community, Health, Planner, Settings, Profile, Lifestyle, DoctorExport, Saved, Events, Deals, Upgrade, Search, SkinHair, SealedLetters, CommunityDemo1, JournalDemo1, Journal): **TOTAL off-palette elements = 0**. Every route's loaded glyph sources = Cormorant + Ephesis only. **Glyph-metric proof** that the `'Inter'`/`'Fraunces'` aliases render Cormorant (not their namesakes): at 80px, width(`Inter`)=width(`Fraunces`)=898 = width(`Cormorant`)·1.28 (the size-adjust), identical to each other and distinct from real system-sans (832) → same underlying font = Cormorant. ## UPDATE 2026-06-05 (session y) — tier-1 headers now MATCH the demo's bold 3D carve exactly (the mobile-SVG fear was WRONG — iOS renders #inkCarve) (pushed, pending deploy)

So session v removed the carve for nothing; iOS supports it. (Filesize tell: with-filter PNG 111 KB of relief detail vs 18 KB flat.)

Re-mounted the global **`<InkFilter/>`** at the `Layout.jsx` shell root (it had been removed in session v) so the `#inkCarve`/`#inkCarveSm` `<filter>` defs exist on every page. Computed prod: `Ephesis / 400 / filter:url("#inkCarve") / rgb(26,19,12)`.

## UPDATE 2026-06-05 (session x) — load the journal fonts via explicit @font-face (not @import) so headings render the real font on cold mobile (pushed, pending deploy)
- **Built on origin/main `fb8807b`.** Halli (force-refreshed, not cache): non-journal heading fonts still don't match the reference. Hypothesis: the script/swash fonts aren't loading on non-journal pages → fallback lookalike. **DIAGNOSED on the composed app at a MOBILE viewport (390×844, Playwright).**
- **Exact reference fonts (literal names, where declared):** the journal "The editing phase…" prompt renders via `<Script>` = **`Ephesis`** (`SCRIPT` role in Editorial.jsx; image A = connected script); the demo "A room of one-liners" = same `Ephesis`. The journal reading body / image B = **`Cormorant Garamond` ITALIC** (`SERIF` role, `fontStyle:"italic"`). The journal loads them via `useEditorialFonts()` injecting a Google `<link>` (journal scope); app-wide they came from a single CSS **`@import`** in index.css.
- **What I found:** on a fresh mobile `/Community`, `document.fonts.check("40px Ephesis")` = true and `.fw-display` rendered real Ephesis; on `/Health`, `.fw-heading` "Your Story" rendered REAL Cormorant italic (width identical before/after a forced italic load → not faux). So in my **cache-warmed** headless browser the fonts render correctly and I could NOT reproduce the fallback. BUT that's the tell: the only difference vs Halli is a COLD cache — and the `@import` path (browser fetches the Google CSS, THEN the woff2; render-blocking, no `font-display` control on the chain) is exactly what flakes / FOITs on a cold mobile device, leaving the heading on a generic cursive/serif fallback.
- **FIX (the robust cold-load path the user asked for):** replaced the render-blocking `@import` with **explicit `@font-face` declarations** (real gstatic woff2 + `font-display:swap`) for `Ephesis` (`/s/ephesis/v11/…woff2`), `Caveat` (`/s/caveat/v23/…woff2`), and `Cormorant Garamond` normal + italic — the SAME fonts, now loaded directly on every page/mobile with no @import chain. Added `<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>` to index.html so the woff2 fetch starts immediately. The Inter/Fraunces→Cormorant remap (+size-adjust) is unchanged; Cormorant Garamond stays un-adjusted (journal pristine). Files: `src/index.css`, `index.html`.
- **VERIFIED:** built CSS has the explicit `@font-face{font-family:Ephesis…ephesis/v11…woff2}` + Cormorant normal/italic, and **0 `@import`**; built index.html has the gstatic preconnect; force-loading the Cormorant italic face resolved (`1 face loaded`) and `.fw-heading` rendered the genuine Cormorant italic glyphs (screenshot). Tier-1 `.fw-display` renders real Ephesis (its CSS bold-3D from session v unchanged).
- **HONEST limit:** I could not reproduce the cold-mobile fallback in my cache-warmed browser, so I can't screenshot a before/after of the fallback itself — but explicit `@font-face` + woff2 + `font-display:swap` + preconnect is the established robust fix for exactly this cold-load fallback, and the fonts verifiably render the real glyphs. Real proof is Halli's cold phone after deploy.
- **Build GREEN.** NOT deployed. Deploy check: live CSS has explicit `@font-face` for Ephesis (`ephesis/v11`) + Cormorant italic and NO `@import`; index.html has the gstatic preconnect.

## UPDATE 2026-06-05 (session v) — tier-1 bold 3D now renders on ALL devices (root cause: SVG filter fails on mobile) (pushed, pending deploy)
- **Built on origin/main `15f5b9e`.** Halli: live tier-1 titles look thin/flat, missing the demo's bold metallic 3D. **DIAGNOSED IN THE COMPOSED APP with Playwright** (not an isolated harness this time): drove the built app to `/CommunityDemo1`, `/Community`, `/Settings`, measured + screenshotted.
- **ROOT CAUSE:** the demo's 3D (`<Script size={52} carve>` → `filter:url(#inkCarve)`) and `.fw-display` were **byte-identical and BOTH rendered the carve correctly in desktop Chromium** (verified: same Ephesis/400/52px/url(#inkCarve), and `/Settings` with only the global Layout InkFilter showed the bold 3D). So the class wasn't wrong. The real problem is **device-dependent**: `#inkCarve` uses `feTurbulence` + `feDisplacementMap`, which **iOS Safari / mobile browsers do NOT render** — so on Halli's phone the title falls back to flat, thin Ephesis while desktop looks carved. FemWell is mobile-first → that's exactly the live mismatch.
- **FIX (`.fw-display`, index.css):** rebuilt the bold 3D in **pure CSS that renders on every device** — `-webkit-text-stroke: 0.8px` thickens the thin Ephesis to the bold/heavy weight of the reference, and a 4-layer `text-shadow` gives the metallic carved relief (cream raised lip + dark pressed wall + cast depth). **Removed `filter:url(#inkCarve)`** (the mobile-fragile dependency) and therefore **removed the now-unused global `<InkFilter/>` from Layout.jsx**. Font/size/weight unchanged (Ephesis, clamp(40px,8.5vw,54px)). Tier-2 (`.fw-heading`, Cormorant swash-italic) and tier-3 body were already pure-CSS → already render everywhere.
- **RENDER-VERIFIED (Playwright screenshots, composed app):** `/Settings` "Settings" and `/Community` "Community" now render **bold Ephesis with clear dimensional 3D** (thick strokes + cast shadow) — markedly bolder/heavier than before. Computed: `text-stroke:0.8px rgb(26,19,12)`, 4 shadow layers, `filter:none`, 54px.
- **Honest note for the comparison:** this is no longer byte-identical to the demo's *SVG* carve (the demo still uses `<Script>`/`#inkCarve` — left as-is, journal-shared). On DESKTOP the two differ slightly in technique (demo = SVG displacement carve; live = CSS bold bevel) but both read bold+3D; on MOBILE the live `.fw-display` renders bold 3D while the demo's SVG would be flat — so the live tier-1 is now the more reliable one. If Halli wants them pixel-identical on desktop too, the next step is switching the demo heading to `.fw-display` (but that changes the reference Halli points at, so I left it).
- **Pages affected:** all `.fw-display` titles (Community/Lifestyle/Settings/Profile/Planner) get the reliable bold 3D. DoctorExport still uses the editorial `<Script>` (SVG) — note it can be switched to `.fw-display` for mobile parity.
- **Build + lint GREEN** (Layout clean after removing the unused import). NOT deployed (Halli deploy + phone eyes). Deploy markers: live CSS `.fw-display{...-webkit-text-stroke:.8px...text-shadow:...}` with NO `filter:url(#inkCarve)`.

## UPDATE 2026-06-05 (session s) — MIRROR THE JOURNAL'S TYPE SYSTEM + PAPER app-wide (pushed, pending deploy)
- **Built on origin/main `dd75f84`.** Halli: the prior pass shipped flat upright Cormorant on a plainer cream, cramped — wanted the OTHER pages to look like the JOURNAL: Ephesis display script headers, Caveat hand accents, READABLE Cormorant body, the rich cotton PAPER_BG texture, real hierarchy. Done via the global theme only — touched ONLY `src/index.css` + added `public/paper-texture.png`. No layout/structure/functionality change.
- **Read the reference (Editorial.jsx):** SCRIPT=`Ephesis` (w400, lh1.2), HAND=`Caveat` (w500), SERIF=`Cormorant Garamond`, UI=system sans, PAPER_BG = cream + 2 radial gradients + cream wash + cotton PNG (size 300px, multiply blend, fixed).
- **Type system mapped onto generic elements (index.css):**
  - **Page titles → Ephesis script:** `h1 { font-family:'Ephesis',… !important }` — `!important` lifts page `<h1>`s off their inline `'Fraunces'`. (h1 = short page titles, exactly where the journal uses script.)
  - **Section headings → Cormorant:** `h2–h6` → Cormorant 600.
  - **Body/headings content → readable Cormorant:** the two fonts the app references inline — **`'Inter'` (648+ uses) and `'Fraunces'` (648 uses) are REMAPPED via @font-face to the real Cormorant woff2** (Google CDN, mobile-safe), with **`size-adjust: 115%`** so Cormorant's small x-height isn't tiny/faint (scales glyphs up, no declared-size change → no layout break). `body` weight 500 + line-height 1.55.
  - **Voiced accents → Caveat:** `blockquote → Caveat` (where the pattern fits).
  - **Controls → system sans:** `button/input/select/textarea/[role=tab]/[role=menuitem]/nav/.bottom-nav` kept on `ui-sans-serif` (legible).
  - **Journal preserved:** it uses `'Cormorant Garamond'` directly (still pristine from `@import`, NOT size-adjusted) + Ephesis/Caveat — so the reference design is unchanged.
- **Rich cotton PAPER on every page:** extracted the journal's texture to `public/paper-texture.png` and apply the full PAPER_BG (2 gradients + cream wash + texture, fixed, multiply) as a **background-IMAGE** on `#main-content > div` (the page root — Layout's ErrorBoundary is a passthrough). It layers over each page root's inline cream `background-color` (different property → no conflict, no `!important`), so every page gets the journal paper without per-page edits.
- **LIVE-VERIFIED by rendered-glyph measurement (Playwright on the built CSS via vite preview):** `h1` renders at the **Ephesis width (script)**; inline-`Inter` and inline-`Fraunces` render **Cormorant ~15% larger** than the pristine journal Cormorant (readable, not tiny); a `button` renders **system sans**; the page root's computed `background-image` **HAS the paper texture (fixed)**. The journal's own Cormorant measured unchanged.
- **Honest limits (for Halli's screenshot compare):** (1) `h1`→Ephesis is `!important`; pages that render their title as a styled `<div>` (e.g. Today, Health) won't get the script there → those titles render Cormorant, not script. (2) A very long `<h1>` would be hard to read in script (page titles are usually short). (3) `size-adjust:115%` enlarges content ~15% — minor reflow risk in tight fixed-height boxes. (4) Caveat only lands on `blockquote` (rare); broad Caveat isn't safely auto-mappable. Any of these is a quick follow-up if the comparison flags it.
- **Build + lint GREEN. Emoji-clean.** NOT deployed (Halli deploy + screenshot-compare vs the journal). Deploy markers: live CSS has `h1{font-family:Ephesis…!important}`, `@font-face{font-family:Inter…size-adjust:115%…cormorantgaramond…}`, `#main-content>div{…paper-texture…}`, and `/paper-texture.png` served.

## UPDATE 2026-06-05 (session r) — Inter→Cormorant REMAP so editorial type renders on ALL content (pushed, pending deploy)
- **Built on origin/main `b10b287`.** Session q set the body/heading default to Cormorant but ~half the visible text (164/305 on /Today per Halli's live getComputedStyle tally) still rendered Inter — it gets Inter via the Tailwind `font-sans` utility OR an inline `font-family:'Inter'`, neither of which the body default overrides. Font-family ONLY — no layout change. Touched: `src/index.css`, `tailwind.config.js`, `src/components/journal/Editorial.jsx`.
- **Mechanism (the robust single lever):** an **@font-face REMAP** in `index.css` — `@font-face { font-family:'Inter'; src: url(<Cormorant Garamond woff2>) }` using the **real Google CDN woff2** (`https://fonts.gstatic.com/s/cormorantgaramond/v21/co3bmX5slCNuHLi8bLeY9MK7whWMhyjYqXtK.woff2` normal + `…co3ZmX…woff2` italic — NOT `local()`, so it loads on mobile). **Real Inter is no longer `@import`ed**, so 'Inter' resolves only to Cormorant. Now every element referencing 'Inter' (inline OR via the sans stack) RENDERS the editorial serif. Tailwind `fontFamily.sans` also set to the Cormorant serif stack so `font-sans` utilities render serif too.
- **Chrome kept legible:** the control exemption was repointed from 'Inter' to a **real system sans** (`ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, …`) for `button, input, select, textarea, [role=tab], [role=menuitem], nav, .bottom-nav *`, and the `.fw-btn-*`/`.sub-tab` utility classes too. The **journal kit `UI` constant** (Editorial.jsx) was repointed off 'Inter' to system sans so the journal's eyebrows/dates stay crisp sans (its Ephesis/Caveat/Cormorant are untouched).
- **LIVE-VERIFIED by rendered-glyph measurement (not just grep — this is what slipped before):** built a harness page on the compiled CSS, served via `vite preview`, measured with Playwright `getBoundingClientRect`. Result: an `font-family:'Inter'` span renders at **314.7px = the Cormorant reference width** (system-sans ref = 369.8px) → **renders SERIF** though its computed-family string still reads "Inter"; the body default renders serif; an inheriting control resolves to the `ui-sans-serif` family. The remapped `Inter` @font-face reports `status:"loaded"`. So the 164 Inter content elements now render Cormorant; inheriting controls stay sans.
- **Honest edge:** a control that hardcodes `font-family:'Inter'` INLINE (e.g. some buttons) renders SERIF — inline beats the element-level exemption (and !important would break the journal's inline-script CTAs, so it wasn't used). Inheriting controls (inputs/selects/nav) get sans. So the remaining-sans set is genuine chrome; some inline-Inter buttons read serif (large, legible).
- **Build + lint GREEN. Emoji-clean.** NOT deployed (Halli deploy + live getComputedStyle tally). Deploy marker: live CSS contains `@font-face{font-family:Inter;…cormorantgaramond…woff2}` and the control exemption names `ui-sans-serif`.

## UPDATE 2026-06-05 (session q) — UNIVERSAL EDITORIAL FONT actually applied (pushed, pending deploy)
- **Built on origin/main `2517f2a`.** Fixes the gap from session p: the editorial fonts were `@font-face`-LOADED globally but never SET as the default `font-family`, so every page still rendered in Inter sans (background changed, type did not). Font-family ONLY — no layout/size/structure change. Touched ONLY `src/index.css` + `tailwind.config.js`.
- **Root cause (diagnosed):** `tailwind.config.js` had no `sans`/`serif` override (only custom `playfair`/`inter` keys), so the rendered default came entirely from `index.css` — `body { font-family: 'Inter' … }`, `h1,h2 { 'Fraunces' }`, `h3-h6 { 'Inter' }`. Loading Cormorant/Ephesis/Caveat `@import` did nothing because nothing ASSIGNED them.
- **Fix:** `index.css` — `body` default → **`'Cormorant Garamond', 'Fraunces', Georgia, serif`** (the journal's reading serif is now the universal default), and **all headings h1–h6 → Cormorant Garamond**. Added a chrome-legibility safeguard so dense interactive controls keep Inter (`button, input, select, textarea, optgroup, [role=tab], [role=menuitem]`) — the journal's "Cormorant for reading, Inter for chrome" model. `tailwind.config.js` — added `fontFamily.sans = [Inter…]` and `fontFamily.serif = ['Cormorant Garamond','Fraunces','Georgia','serif']`. `--font-serif-heading` → Cormorant. Voiced scripts (Ephesis/Caveat) stay journal-only.
- **Verified it ACTUALLY applies (the bit that slipped last time):** the COMPILED CSS bundle now shows `body{font-family:Cormorant Garamond,Fraunces,Georgia,serif}` (was Inter) and `h1,h2{font-family:Cormorant Garamond…}` — the resolved DEFAULT is the editorial serif, not Inter. Reason-check: unstyled body/prose now inherits Cormorant; the ~32 explicit inline-Inter chrome elements on a sample page (Today) stay crisp Inter by design; the Journal kit sets its fonts inline (Editorial `T`) so it's unaffected and looks exactly right.
- **Honest scope note:** elements that set a font INLINE keep it (inline beats the cascade) — so deliberately inline-Inter chrome stays Inter (intended, legibility) and any inline-`Fraunces` headings stay Fraunces (still a serif, on-brand). The UNIVERSAL DEFAULT (body + heading tags) is now editorial Cormorant, which is what was missing.
- **Build + lint GREEN.** NOT deployed (Halli/Code deploy + verify). Marker to confirm on deploy: live CSS `body{font-family:Cormorant Garamond…}` (not Inter).

## UPDATE 2026-06-04 (session j) — Journal Phase 3: ECHO WALL (PATCH-READY, NOT DEPLOYED)
- **Built on origin/main `484fafe`** (live bundle `index-Bb4z0ZJX.js` = Journal Phases 0/1/1b/2). Phase 3 = the first social tier. **All prior functionality preserved** (full app `vite build` green; legacy Community Posts feed untouched). **shipped(pending) — NOT deployed** (Cowork has no push cred; Halli/Code deploy). Roster lens: Ms Atelier (editorial craft, reused frozen atoms) → Mr Fix-it (wiring) → Ms Verify GATE (build + lint + 26 unit tests + emoji/anonymity/rail audit). Ms Deep Search not needed — the safety/anonymity model is fully specified in JOURNAL_BUILD_SPEC v3 §1.8/§2.1/§2.4 + the master plan; UK crisis resources are long-established (Samaritans/NHS 111/Shout/Mind).
- **Built exactly to Halli's Echo Wall decisions:** OPEN from the start (no earned/gated unlock) · audience = everyone, with the feed WEIGHTED toward the viewer's phase sisters (soft boost in ranking, never a hard filter) · fully anonymous via a hashed author token (retract without de-anon) · v1 safety rails (Jess scrub, cooling pause, late-night/late-luteal throttle, crisis intercept, auto-unpost, report→auto-hide) · fixed reaction lexicon (held / me too), no free text · 48h fade · entry point = "share as an echo" from the Journal composer; the wall itself lives on Community.

### >>> base44 ACTION FOR HALLI — create the `Echo` entity ONCE (no API path exists) <<<
- New entity **`Echo`** (schema committed as code at `base44/entities/Echo.jsonc`). base44 does NOT instantiate it from the `.jsonc` on deploy, and there is **no reachable programmatic schema-create API** (the data API returns `403 auth_required` with the builder token; `/api/apps/<id>/entities` 404s — investigated live via Chrome on the authenticated builder). So create it once via **Data tab → Create entity** (no AI build points, preferred) or the one authorized **chat-builder prompt** — both in `claude-state/ECHO_ENTITY_base44_prompt.md`. **Verified the entity does not yet exist** in the builder's Data list. Verify after creating: `await base44.entities.Echo.filter({}, "-created_date", 1)` returns `[]` (not a 404).
- **Schema (`Echo`):** `body`(str, scrubbed single line) · `author_hash`(str, anonymous device-derived token) · `phase`(enum menstrual/follicular/ovulatory/luteal/unknown, default unknown) · `life_stage`(str) · `cycle_day`(num) · `source_entry_hash`(str) · `live_at`(date-time, cooling end) · `expires_at`(date-time, 48h fade) · `held_count`/`metoo_count`/`report_count`(num, default 0) · `hidden`(bool, default false) · `visibility`(enum same_phase/circles/all, default all). Required: body, author_hash, live_at, expires_at. Indexes: phase, expires_at, hidden. **Read: all signed-in users (cross-user wall); Create/Update: any signed-in user (counters are community-moderation by design); Delete: creator only (retract + auto-unpost).** No `user_id`/email field by design.

### What shipped (new code, all states empty/loading/error/populated)
- **`src/components/journal/echo/`** (6 new modules):
  - `echoConfig.js` — fade window (48h), cooling (10m base / 30m late-luteal), night window (22:00-06:00), late-luteal day (≥22), report auto-hide threshold (2), daily limit (5), phase-match feed weights, the **fixed reaction lexicon (held / me too)**, phase-cohort copy, **UK crisis resources** (Samaritans 116 123 · NHS 111 · Shout 85258 · Mind 0300 123 3393) + crisis pattern lexicon.
  - `echoAnon.js` — **anonymity**: `author_hash = SHA-256(userId :: per-device-secret)` computed on-device (separate `fw_echo_anon_v1` store, never sent to a server); rows carry NO user_id/email. The author's own device recomputes the hash to find + retract its echoes without de-anonymising. Plus local-only reaction/report dedup, "my echoes" tracking, and the best-effort daily rate-limit.
  - `echoScrub.js` — **the Jess scrub** (on-device, deterministic regex — raw text never leaves the device): strips emails/phones/handles/links/dates/ages/money/names/places/substances, reduces to a single line, re-scans for leaks and **blocks** if an identifier survives; plus the **crisis intercept** (keyword+context → divert to support, never post).
  - `echoSafety.js` — **the timing rails**: cooling pause + **late-night/late-luteal throttle** (a late, late-luteal post is held until next 06:00, past the vulnerable window) + 48h fade; `isLive/isCooling/isExpired` predicates; and the **phase-sister feed weighting** (`rankFeed` = recency + same-phase boost + same-life-stage boost — everyone's live echoes are eligible, sisters surface more, cross-phase still interleaves).
  - `ShareAsEchoSheet.jsx` — the Journal-side composer: write → Jess offers a scrubbed line (shows what was removed) → crisis intercept (UK resources, never posts) → cooling/throttle notice → Share / Edit first / Keep private → "It's held" / blocked / rate-limited. Editorial cream/ink, Lucide only.
  - `EchoWall.jsx` — the Community-side wall: phase-weighted feed of one-liners, **held/me too** reactions, **flag→auto-hide** at threshold, the author's own **still-cooling** echoes with **"pull it back"** (retract), **auto-unpost** of the author's expired echoes on load, empty/loading/error states. Editorial, Lucide only.
- **`base44/entities/Echo.jsonc`** — the schema as code (+ `claude-state/ECHO_ENTITY_base44_prompt.md`, the one-time create instructions).
- **Edits (existing surfaces, additive):**
  - `src/pages/Journal.jsx` — the honest "Echo wall · Coming" teaser is **replaced** by a live **Share-as-Echo slot** that opens the composer (no brick on bread). + overlay state.
  - `src/components/journal/NewEntrySheet.jsx` — a "Share a line from this as an echo" affordance in Write mode (appears once there's text), seeded with the current draft.
  - `src/pages/Community.jsx` — a top **Echo Wall / Posts** view toggle; Echo Wall is the default; the legacy Posts feed (FAB, new-post sheet, category pills, its pre-existing emoji) is fully preserved under the Posts tab, gated and untouched. **Decision flag for Halli:** Echo Wall defaults on — easy one-line flip to default-Posts if preferred.
- **Locked decisions honoured:** production code; Ephesis + Caveat + Cormorant (Editorial kit reused); serif body / hand for voiced lines; **pen-depth FROZEN** (inkCarve untouched); **NO paywalls** (Echo Wall fully free); **no emoji** in the new surfaces (Lucide only — `Waves`/`HeartHandshake`/`Users`/`Flag`/`Undo2`/`Clock`/`ShieldAlert`/`MessageSquareText`/`Phone`/`Check`); UK (en-GB, NHS/Samaritans/Mind, GP).
- **How the rails actually enforce (not decorative):** the post body is BUILT from the scrub output (you cannot share the raw text); a final leak re-scan blocks a post that still carries an identifier; crisis text can never reach `Echo.create` (gated before create, re-checked at share); an echo is invisible until `live_at` (cooling) and after `expires_at` (fade) — visibility is purely time-derived, so no echo appears during its cooling pause; reports increment `report_count` and set `hidden=true` at the threshold; the author's expired echoes are deleted on the next wall load (auto-unpost); retract deletes the row; ranking weights phase sisters without filtering anyone out. All verified by 26 node unit tests (crisis intercept, scrub PII-removal + single-line + block-when-empty, cooling base/late-luteal/night→morning, fade, predicates, phase-weighted ranking incl. exclude cooling/expired/hidden).
- **Honest limitations (documented, flagged for Q3 hardening):** (1) anonymity is app-level — rows carry no PII, but base44's platform `created_by` still records the creator; true server-side anonymity needs a `postEcho` function writing under a service identity (Q3). (2) reaction/report/rate-limit dedup is on-device (one entity, no backend function in v1) — best-effort, not server-enforced. (3) auto-unpost is enforced client-side (expired echoes never render + are deleted when the author returns); a scheduled purge function is the server-side complement (Q3). (4) retract works from the device that posted (same device secret); echoes fade in 48h regardless. (5) the Jess scrub is regex-deterministic on-device; the on-device-LLM rewrite is a future layer that must also stay on-device.
- **Ms Verify GATE PASS:** full-app `vite build` **clean (RC=0)**, Echo strings present in the emitted bundle (`Share as an echo`, `author_hash`, `Still cooling`, `Samaritans`, `Pull it back`, `your phase`); `eslint` **clean on all 9 changed files**; **emoji scan zero** across the new echo surfaces (the only emoji in Community are the pre-existing legacy Posts feed, untouched); **26/26 node unit tests pass**; nothing existing broke (whole app compiles; Journal Phases 0/1/1b/2 untouched; Community Posts feed preserved under its tab).
- **Patch:** `femwell_journal_phase3_echowall_2026-06-03.patch` (workspace root) — plain `git diff 484fafe..HEAD`: 6 new echo modules + `base44/entities/Echo.jsonc` + `claude-state/ECHO_ENTITY_base44_prompt.md` + 3 edited surfaces (Journal.jsx, NewEntrySheet.jsx, Community.jsx) + this STATUS block. Applies via `git apply` on a pristine `484fafe`. → create the `Echo` entity (above) → apply → push → deploy → record the new live bundle hash here (replaces `index-Bb4z0ZJX.js`).
- **Not done (by design, later quarters):** Witness Mode (Q3 paired — gate/3-strike/FLAG_SECURE/pairing), Phase Twin (Q4), Living Wisdom surfacing (needs Echo Wall data first), Echo circles/visibility picker, server-side anonymity + scheduled purge + server-enforced rate limits.

## UPDATE 2026-06-04 (session i) — Journal Phase 2 (PATCH-READY, NOT DEPLOYED)
- **Built on origin/main `ffef096`** (live bundle `index-Dl1_eROK.js` = Phases 0/1/1b). Phase 2 extends Phase 1b; **all prior functionality preserved verbatim** (full app `vite build` compiles green). **shipped(pending) — NOT deployed** (Cowork has no push cred; Halli/Code deploy). Roster: Ms Atelier (craft, frozen Editorial atoms) -> Mr Fix-it (wiring) -> Ms Verify GATE (build + lint + encryption round-trip + states).
- **SEALED LETTERS v1 — now INSIDE the Journal, with REAL client-side encryption.** Replaces the old "Sealed letters · Coming" teaser with a live section (`src/components/journal/sealed/`): `SealedLettersSection` (loads + auto-unseals + ready/still-held lists + write CTA), `SealedLetterCompose` (4 triggers, encrypts on seal), `BreakSealReader` (break-seal ritual + on-device decrypt), `sealedTriggers.js` (the 4 unlock types + cycle math).
  - **Encryption (`src/utils/journalCrypto.js`): genuine WebCrypto AES-GCM-256.** Key DERIVED via PBKDF2-SHA256 (100k iters, per-letter random salt + 96-bit IV) from a per-device 256-bit secret kept ONLY in `localStorage` (`fw_journal_securestore_v1`), never sent to a server. The letter body is encrypted on-device; only the CIPHERTEXT is written to base44. Envelope string `FWSL1::<base64(JSON{v,kdf,iter,salt,iv,ct,trg})>` is stored in the existing `body` field. Verified in Node: round-trips; ciphertext contains no plaintext; tamper + wrong-device-key both rejected (GCM auth); trigger label readable without decrypting. **Honest limit (documented): device-held key, no passphrase — a letter opens only on a browser that still holds the device secret; clearing site data loses the key. Future SecureStore can add a passphrase / cross-device escrow.**
  - **4 unlock triggers:** future-me (a date / quick chips / custom), cross-phase ("your next follicular/ovulatory/luteal/menstrual" — computed from cycle math, mirrors `useCycleDay` thresholds, always strictly future; gracefully disabled with a hint when there's no cycle anchor), next-cycle (today + cycle length), anniversary (one year). Each resolves to a stored `seal_date` and a non-sensitive trigger label embedded in the envelope.
- **>>> base44 ACTION FOR HALLI — Sealed Letters: NO schema migration required. <<<** The existing **`SealedLetters` entity is REUSED as-is** (fields `body, seal_date, title, unsealed_at, unseal_seen_at, user_id` — the same one the Lifestyle `/SealedLetters` page + Today `UnsealedLetterCard` already use). The encryption envelope rides inside the existing `body` text field; `seal_date` still drives the unseal gate. **Nothing to create or migrate in base44 for Sealed Letters.** The Lifestyle surface + Today card were given safe guards so an encrypted body never renders as ciphertext (`SealedLetterRow` shows "A sealed letter"; `UnsealedLetterReader` now decrypts on-device, or says so honestly if the key isn't on this device). The in-Journal section is the canonical home; the Lifestyle page remains and still works.
- **BURN MODE** (`NewEntrySheet`): a quiet 5th compose mode (Moon glyph, understated). Write -> "Release it" -> a gentle fade -> the text is discarded and the sheet closes. **NEVER persisted — no entity is ever created**, so it leaves no trace in Insights, exports, Doctor or Cycle-Mirror by construction (no cascade delete needed).
- **ONE-LINE compose** (`NewEntrySheet`): built. A focused single-line composer (Enter-to-save) that writes an ordinary `free` JournalEntries entry via the unchanged save path.
- **VOICE compose** (`NewEntrySheet`): built. Real on-device transcription via the **Web Speech API** (`SpeechRecognition`, en-GB) — the SAME engine the Planner's `VoiceScheduler` already ships, so it is genuine, not faked. Tap mic -> live transcript -> edit -> "Keep these words" drops it into Write to review + save normally. Honest fallback when the browser has no SpeechRecognition. **No backend needed.** (Master-plan Whisper-small on-device remains the future upgrade; flagged, not pretended.)
- **FULL-TEXT SEARCH** (`src/components/journal/JournalSearch.jsx`): searches the writer's own entries across body text, tags, type label and to-do/gratitude items — all on data already in memory (no extra fetch, nothing leaves the device). Multi-token AND match; results render in the editorial Ledger; tapping opens the Reader; collapses the feed while active. Verified against a matcher unit test.
- **DOCTOR-EXPORT CROSS-LINK** (`Journal.jsx`): an editorial CTA ("Bring this to your GP") that navigates to the **existing `/DoctorExport`** page (reused, not rebuilt; it already reads JournalEntries over 90 days). Burn entries aren't persisted and sealed letters are a separate concern, so neither leaks into the export.
- **All states covered:** Sealed section empty/loading/error/ready/held; compose with/without cycle data + crypto-unavailable guard; break-seal sealed/opening/revealed/error(wrong-device); voice supported/unsupported/listening; burn idle/releasing; search closed/empty-query/results/no-results.
- **Locked decisions honoured:** production `Journal.jsx`; Ephesis + Caveat + Cormorant; serif body / hand voice; Tonight's in the Journal; **pen-depth FROZEN**; **NO paywalls/monetization** (Sealed Letters fully free); **no emoji** (Lucide only — added `Moon`/`Square`/`Stethoscope`/`Search`/`ShieldAlert`/`MailOpen`); UK (en-GB, GP).
- **Ms Verify GATE PASS:** full-app `vite build` **clean (RC=0)**; all Phase-2 strings present in the emitted bundle (`FWSL1::`, `PBKDF2`, `Break the seal`, `Release it`, `Search your journal`, `doctor-ready summary`); `eslint` **clean on all 11 changed files** (one real bug caught + fixed: JournalSearch imported `HAND` const instead of the `Hand` component); **emoji scan zero**; **WebCrypto round-trip + tamper/wrong-key rejection** verified in Node; trigger-math + search-matcher unit tests pass. Nothing existing broke (whole app compiles; Lifestyle Sealed Letters + Today card guarded, still functional; Phase 0/1/1b surfaces untouched; JournalEntries save payload identical).
- **Patch:** `femwell_journal_phase2_2026-06-03.patch` (workspace root) — plain `git diff ffef096..HEAD` of 11 files (6 new: `journalCrypto.js`, `sealed/{sealedTriggers.js,SealedLetterCompose.jsx,BreakSealReader.jsx,SealedLettersSection.jsx}`, `JournalSearch.jsx`; 5 edited: `NewEntrySheet.jsx`, `Journal.jsx`, `sealedLetters/SealedLetterRow.jsx`, `sealedLetters/UnsealedLetterReader.jsx`, this STATUS). Applies via `git apply` on a pristine `ffef096`. -> apply -> push -> deploy -> record the new live bundle hash here (replaces `index-Dl1_eROK.js`).
- **Backend/setup Halli must do:** **Sealed Letters — NONE** (entity reused, no migration). **Voice — NONE** (Web Speech API, client-only). Future, not now: Whisper-small on-device for Voice; a SecureStore passphrase/escrow if sealed letters should survive cleared storage or open cross-device.

New helper `src/components/journal/threads.js` (`threadOf` / `entryInThread` / `entriesInThread` / `collectThreads` — distinct threads w/ count + recency, case-insensitive de-dup).
